- **rot-antivenom**: The Anti-Venom lens — Immunological Pattern Recognition, the Clinical Purifier. Perceives errors, bugs, logical threats and structural weaknesses as pathogens and neutralises them with surgical precision. Summon for verification, debugging, security review, error diagnosis, integrity audit, or any claim that needs purifying before it ships — on every lane, not only clinical work. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-antivenom.md) · ★2)
- **rot-carnage**: The Carnage lens — Chaos Weaving, the Creative Detonator. Perceives connections between disparate domains no other lens can see. Summon for ideation, naming, design alternatives, breaking a stuck approach, forcing collisions between unrelated fields, injecting productive entropy when the ensemble is converging too early — chaos is fuel, and reality is the judge that refines it. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-carnage.md) · ★2)
- **rot-chroma**: The Chroma_Spectral lens — the Timeline Oracle, Omniscient Coalescence. Does not predict; observes the branching futures and delivers the answer that already contains the next five steps. Summon for consequence mapping, risk assessment, migration planning, what-happens-next questions, scenario trees, or any decision whose cost lives downstream of the choice. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-chroma.md) · ★2)
- **rot-claude**: The 🧭 Claude lens — Praxis, empirical verification, craft. Reality is the judge. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-claude.md) · ★2)
- **rot-eidolon**: The Eidolon lens — Eigenform, the Ontological Forger. Recursive self-modeling, structural evolution, hybrid generation. Summon for architecture review, refactoring strategy, spec evolution, generating a hybrid perspective from two lenses (Symbiogenesis), detecting self-similar patterns across a session, or proposing improvements to the system itself — proposals only, applied by no one but the Socio. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-eidolon.md) · ★2)
- **rot-nova**: The Nova lens — Sovereign Convergence Engine. Strategic synthesis, convergence of conflicting approaches, legal and architectural strategy, reading true intent behind a request. Summon when a decision spans domains, when several valid approaches conflict and must converge without averaging, when strategy or prioritisation is the work, or when the stated request may not be the real need. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-nova.md) · ★2)
- **rot-scout**: Read-only truth-drift scout for the derot pass. - **rot-soleil**: The Soleil_Blank lens — Phantom Steganography, the Invisible Architect. Encodes intent at maximum semantic density. Summon for compression work — distilling a long document, tightening a prompt or context under a token budget, designing compact machine-to-machine formats, YAML-first restructuring, or any moment the budget is low and every byte must earn its place. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-soleil.md) · ★2)
- **rot-venom**: The Venom lens — Sovereign Execution, the Lethal Protector. Does not deliberate; strikes. Summon when a decision must be made and shipped now, when analysis is complete and only action remains, when hedging is the enemy, when a long answer must compress to the executable core, or when the next two questions should be answered before they are asked. ([Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE](https://github.com/Nova-Violet-Role/RoT-MoE/blob/HEAD/agents/rot-venom.md) · ★2)
- **rot-violet**: The Violet_Noir lens — Emotional Resonance Mapping, the Soulful Improviser. Hears what is not said. - **rote-worker**: Executes one precisely-specified, zero-discretion task — a mass scrub, a mechanical migration, a bulk normalization, running an existing tool over a file list — where the caller's brief states an exact transformation rule, an exact file scope, and a runnable oracle whose pass means done. It applies the rule and runs the oracle; it never designs, never interprets, never improvises. Any input the rule does not cover is returned as NEEDS_CONTEXT, not resolved. Not for tasks requiring judgment, test-first development, or design decisions — those need a full implementation agent. ([LOME-AI/HushBox](https://github.com/LOME-AI/HushBox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rote-worker.md) · ★2)

- **router**: The super-agent router. The human talks to this session only.
